SELECT      C.customer_id,      C.customer_first_name,      C.customer_last_name,      P.product_name,      P.product_price FROM      Customers C JOIN      Orders O ON C.customer_id = O.customer_id JOIN      Invoices I ON O.invoice_number = I.invoice_number JOIN      Order_Items OI ON O.order_id = OI.order_id JOIN      Products P ON OI.product_id = P.product_id JOIN      Shipment_Items SI ON OI.order_item_id = SI.order_item_id JOIN      Shipments S ON SI.shipment_id = S.shipment_id WHERE      O.order_status_code = 'Paid' ORDER BY      P.product_price DESC;
SELECT    o.order_id,    SUM(p.product_price) as sum FROM    Orders o   JOIN    Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id   JOIN    Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY    o.order_id ORDER BY    o.order_id ASC;
SELECT      SUM(CASE WHEN invoice_status_code = 'Paid' THEN product_price END) AS total_cost,      SUM(CASE WHEN invoice_status_code = 'Paid' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS total_orders FROM      Invoices WHERE      invoice_status_code IN ('Paid', 'Issued') GROUP BY      SUM(CASE WHEN invoice_status_code = 'Paid' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END);

SELECT    o.order_status_code,    s.shipment_tracking_number FROM    orders o   JOIN shipments s ON o.order_id = s.order_id   JOIN order_items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id   JOIN shipments_items si ON oi.order_item_id = si.order_item_id   JOIN products p ON si.product_id = p.product_id WHERE    o.order_status_code IN ('Ordered', 'Shipped')   AND p.product_name LIKE '%Shipment%'   AND p.product_price > 0 ORDER BY    o.order_status_code,    p.product_name;
SELECT    i.order_status_code,    si.shipment_tracking_number FROM    Invoices i   JOIN Shipment_Items si ON i.invoice_number = si.invoice_number   JOIN Orders o ON si.order_id = o.order_id   JOIN Products p ON o.product_id = p.product_id WHERE    i.invoice_status_code IN ('Ordered', 'Shipped') AND    o.order_status_code IN ('Cancelled', 'Completed')   AND p.product_name LIKE '%Shipment%'   AND i.invoice_date BETWEEN '2018-01-01' AND '2018-03-31';
SELECT      P.product_name,      P.product_color FROM      Products P WHERE      P.product_id IN (         SELECT              P1.product_id         FROM              Products P1         JOIN              Order_Items OI ON P1.product_id = OI.product_id         JOIN              Shipment_Items S ON OI.order_item_id = S.order_item_id         JOIN              Invoices I ON S.invoice_number = I.invoice_number         JOIN              Orders O ON I.order_id = O.order_id         WHERE              I.invoice_status_code = 'Paid' AND O.order_status_code = 'Paid'     ) ORDER BY      P.product_id,      P.product_name,      O.order_id,      P.order_item_id;
SELECT      C.customer_first_name,      C.customer_last_name,      P.product_name,      P.product_price,      P.product_color,      P.product_size,      P.product_description  FROM      Customers C  INNER JOIN      Products P ON C.customer_id = P.customer_id  WHERE      C.customer_id IN (         SELECT              customer_id          FROM              Shipment_Items          GROUP BY              customer_id          HAVING              COUNT(*) > 1     )      AND      P.product_id IN (         SELECT              product_id          FROM              Order_Items          GROUP BY              product_id          HAVING              COUNT(*) > 1     )      AND      C.customer_id IN (         SELECT              customer_id          FROM              Orders          GROUP BY              customer_id          HAVING              COUNT(*) > 1     )      AND      P.product_id IN (         SELECT              product_id          FROM              Invoices          GROUP BY              product_id          HAVING              COUNT(*) > 1     );
SELECT      p.product_name,      p.product_price,      p.product_description  FROM      Products p  JOIN      (SELECT           i.product_id,           p.product_id,           i.order_id,           i.order_item_status_code       FROM           Order_Items i       JOIN           Invoices o ON i.order_id = o.order_id       JOIN           Customers c ON i.customer_id = c.customer_id       WHERE           o.invoice_status_code = 'Paid'       GROUP BY           i.product_id,           p.product_id,           i.order_id,           i.order_item_status_code)      AS subquery  ON      p.product_id = subquery.product_id  ORDER BY      p.product_name;
SELECT      p.product_name,      p.product_price,      p.product_description FROM      Products p WHERE      p.product_id IN (         SELECT oi.product_id          FROM Order_Items oi          JOIN Invoices i ON oi.product_id = i.product_id          WHERE i.invoice_status_code = 'Paid'     ) ORDER BY      p.product_id;
SELECT    oi.product_name,    oi.product_price,    oi.order_item_status_code FROM    Invoices i   JOIN Order_Items oi ON i.invoice_number = oi.invoice_number   JOIN Shipment_Items si ON oi.order_item_id = si.order_item_id WHERE    i.invoice_status_code!= 'Shipped'   AND i.invoice_number NOT IN (     SELECT invoice_number      FROM Shipment_Items      WHERE shipment_date > (SELECT MAX(shipment_date) FROM Shipment_Items)   )
SELECT      i.product_name,      i.product_price,      oi.order_item_status_code FROM      Invoices i JOIN      Orders o ON i.invoice_number = o.invoice_number JOIN      Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id JOIN      Shipment_Items si ON oi.order_item_id = si.order_item_id WHERE      i.invoice_status_code = 'Unshipped' GROUP BY      i.product_name,      i.product_price,      oi.order_item_status_code ORDER BY      i.product_name,      i.product_price,      oi.order_item_status_code;
SELECT      o.order_id,      o.date,      SUM(oi.product_price) AS total_cost FROM      Orders o JOIN      Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id JOIN      Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY      o.order_id,      o.date ORDER BY      o.order_id,      o.date;
SELECT    o.order_id,    o.date_order_placed,    SUM(oi.product_price) AS total_amount_paid FROM    Orders o   JOIN    Shipment_Items si ON o.order_id = si.order_id   JOIN    Products p ON si.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY    o.order_id,    o.date_order_placed ORDER BY    o.order_id,    o.date_order_placed;

SELECT      driver.Name FROM      driver  INNER JOIN      vehicle_driver ON driver.Driver_ID = vehicle_driver.Driver_ID  WHERE      driver.Citizenship = 'United States';
SELECT    driver.Name  FROM    driver  WHERE    driver.Citizenship = 'United States';
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT Driver_ID) AS "Number of Vehicles",      Driver_ID  FROM      vehicle_driver  WHERE      Driver_ID IN (         SELECT              Driver_ID          FROM              vehicle          GROUP BY              Driver_ID          HAVING              COUNT(DISTINCT Vehicle_ID) = (                 SELECT                      COUNT(DISTINCT Vehicle_ID)                  FROM                      vehicle                  GROUP BY                      Vehicle_ID                  HAVING                      COUNT(DISTINCT Driver_ID) = 3             )     )
SELECT    driver_name,    COUNT(DISTINCT vehicle_id) AS num_vehicles FROM    vehicle_driver GROUP BY    driver_name ORDER BY    num_vehicles DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT      AVG(vehicle.Power) AS Average_Power FROM      vehicle  JOIN      vehicle_driver ON vehicle.Vehicle_ID = vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID GROUP BY      driver.Name ORDER BY      Average_Power DESC;
SELECT      MAX(Total_Production) AS Max_Power,      AVG(Total_Production) AS Average_Power FROM      vehicle  WHERE      Vehicle_ID IN (SELECT Vehicle_ID FROM vehicle_driver);
SELECT    v.Vehicle_ID,    COUNT(DISTINCT v.Vehicle_ID) AS vehicle_count FROM    vehicle_driver vd   JOIN vehicle v ON vd.Vehicle_ID = v.Vehicle_ID GROUP BY    v.Vehicle_ID ORDER BY    vehicle_count ASC;
SELECT    MIN(vehicle_model) AS min_model,    MIN(vehicle_build_year) AS min_build_year,    MIN(vehicle_power) AS min_power,    MIN(vehicle_builder) AS min_builder,    MIN(vehicle_total_production) AS min_production FROM    vehicle WHERE    vehicle_driver.driver_id = driver_id;
SELECT    vehicle.Top_Speed,    vehicle.Power FROM    vehicle    JOIN vehicle_driver ON vehicle.Vehicle_ID = vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID    WHERE    vehicle.Build_Year = '1996';
SELECT    v.Model,    v.Top_Speed,    v.Power,    v.Build_Year FROM    vehicle v WHERE    v.Build_Year = '1996';
SELECT      v.Model,      v.Build_Year,      v.Builder  FROM      vehicle v  JOIN      vehicle_driver vd ON v.Vehicle_ID = vd.Vehicle_ID;
SELECT      driver.Name,      vehicle.Model,      vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID FROM      driver  INNER JOIN      vehicle  ON      driver.Driver_ID = vehicle.Driver_ID AND vehicle.Vehicle_ID = vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID INNER JOIN      vehicle_driver  ON      driver.Driver_ID = vehicle_driver.Driver_ID;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT vehicle.Vehicle_ID) AS num_drivers FROM vehicle_driver AS T1 JOIN vehicle AS T2 ON T1.Vehicle_ID = T2.Vehicle_ID WHERE T1.Citizenship = 'United States' AND T1.Racing_Series = 'NASCAR';
SELECT DISTINCT driver_name FROM driver WHERE driver_citizenship = 'United States' JOIN vehicle ON driver.driver_id = vehicle.driver_id ORDER BY driver_name LIMIT 1;
SELECT COUNT(Driver_ID) AS "Number of Drivers" FROM driver WHERE Racing_Series = 'NASCAR';
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT driver_name) AS num_drivers FROM driver WHERE racing_series = 'NASCAR';
SELECT AVG(T1.Top_Speed) AS Average_Vehicle_Speed FROM driver AS T1 JOIN vehicle_driver AS T2 ON T1.Driver_ID = T2.Driver_ID GROUP BY T1.Name ORDER BY T1.Top_Speed DESC;
SELECT AVG(T1.Top_Speed) AS Average_Speed FROM vehicle AS T1   INNER JOIN vehicle_driver AS T2 ON T1.Vehicle_ID = T2.Vehicle_ID   INNER JOIN driver AS T3 ON T2.Driver_ID = T3.Driver_ID GROUP BY T1.Vehicle_ID ORDER BY T1.Top_Speed DESC;
SELECT DISTINCT Driver_ID  FROM vehicle_driver  WHERE Vehicle_ID > 5000;
SELECT    driver.Name  FROM    driver  WHERE    driver.Driver_ID > 5000 ORDER BY    driver.Name;
SELECT    vehicle.Model,    vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID,    vehicle_driver.Driver_ID FROM    vehicle    JOIN vehicle_driver ON vehicle.Vehicle_ID = vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID WHERE    vehicle.Top_Speed > 150 AND    vehicle.Total_Production > 100;
SELECT    vehicle.Model FROM    vehicle WHERE    Total_Production > 100 OR Top_Speed > 150;
SELECT    v.Model,    v.Build_Year FROM    vehicle v WHERE    v.Model LIKE '%DJ%';
SELECT      'driver' AS table_name,     'model' AS column_name,      'build_year' AS column_name,      COUNT(*) AS total_production,      COUNT(DISTINCT 'driver_id') AS driver_id,      COUNT(DISTINCT'vehicle_id') AS vehicle_id  FROM      vehicle_driver  WHERE     'model' LIKE '%DJ%'  GROUP BY      'driver_id',     'vehicle_id' ORDER BY      'build_year',      'driver_id',     'vehicle_id';
SELECT DISTINCT driver.Name  FROM driver  WHERE driver.Driver_ID NOT IN (SELECT Driver_ID FROM vehicle_driver);
SELECT    driver.Name,    vehicle.Model FROM    driver    JOIN vehicle_driver ON driver.Driver_ID = vehicle_driver.Driver_ID    JOIN vehicle ON vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID = vehicle.Vehicle_ID  WHERE    driver.Citizenship!= 'United States';
SELECT    v.Model,    v.Model FROM    vehicle_driver vd   JOIN vehicle v ON vd.Vehicle_ID = v.Vehicle_ID WHERE    vd.Driver_ID IN (1, 2, 3)   OR v.Build_Year = '2000';
SELECT      driver.Name,      vehicle.Model FROM      driver  INNER JOIN      vehicle_driver ON driver.Driver_ID = vehicle_driver.Driver_ID WHERE      driver.Driver_ID IN (1, 2, 3)      AND vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID IN (1, 2, 3)     AND vehicle.Vehicle_ID = vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID ORDER BY      driver.Name, vehicle.Model;
SELECT      v.Model FROM      vehicle v WHERE      v.Driver_ID > 2 OR v.Name = 'Jeff Gordon';
SELECT    d.Name,    v.Model FROM    driver d   JOIN vehicle_driver vd ON d.Driver_ID = vd.Driver_ID AND vd.Vehicle_ID = v.Vehicle_ID WHERE    d.Name = 'Jeff Gordon'   AND vd.Vehicle_ID IN (     SELECT        Vehicle_ID     FROM        vehicle      WHERE        Top_Speed > 2   ) ORDER BY    d.Name,    v.Model;
SELECT    driver.Name,    COUNT(DISTINCT vehicle.Top_Speed) AS Max_Speed FROM    driver    JOIN vehicle_driver ON driver.Driver_ID = vehicle_driver.Driver_ID    JOIN vehicle ON vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID = vehicle.Top_Speed GROUP BY    driver.Name ORDER BY    Max_Speed DESC;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT vehicle.Vehicle_ID) AS Count_Vehicles FROM driver JOIN vehicle_driver ON driver.Driver_ID = vehicle_driver.Driver_ID JOIN vehicle ON vehicle_driver.Vehicle_ID = vehicle.Vehicle_ID WHERE vehicle.Top_Speed = (SELECT MAX(Top_Speed) FROM vehicle);
SELECT    driver.Name  FROM    driver  ORDER BY    driver.Name;
SELECT      v.Model  FROM      vehicle  WHERE      v.Vehicle_ID = (         SELECT              v1.Model          FROM              vehicle          WHERE              v1.Vehicle_ID = v.Vehicle_ID     )      AND v.Vehicle_ID = (         SELECT              v2.Vehicle_ID          FROM              vehicle_driver          WHERE              v2.Vehicle_ID = v.Vehicle_ID     );
SELECT      driver_name,      COUNT(*) AS num_drivers FROM      driver GROUP BY      driver_name;
SELECT    v.Model,    COUNT(DISTINCT v.Vehicle_ID) AS num_drivers FROM    vehicle v   JOIN vehicle_driver vd ON v.Vehicle_ID = vd.Vehicle_ID GROUP BY    v.Model;
SELECT      driver_name,      vehicle_model  FROM      driver  JOIN      vehicle_driver ON driver_driver.driver_id = driver.driver_id  JOIN      vehicle ON vehicle_driver.vehicle_id = vehicle.vehicle_id  WHERE      vehicle_model = 'DJ1' ORDER BY      driver_name,      vehicle_model;
SELECT      d.Name,      v.Model  FROM      driver d  JOIN      vehicle_driver vd ON d.Driver_ID = vd.Driver_ID  JOIN      vehicle v ON vd.Vehicle_ID = v.Vehicle_ID  WHERE      v.Model = 'DJ1';
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT vehicle_id) AS num_drivers FROM vehicle_driver WHERE vehicle_id IS NOT NULL;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT driver.Name) AS Driver_Count FROM driver WHERE driver.Driver_ID NOT IN (     SELECT Driver_ID     FROM vehicle_driver );
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Exams AS T1 GROUP BY T1.Exam_ID ORDER BY T1.Exam_ID;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT T1.Exam_ID) AS Count_Exams FROM      Exams T1 JOIN      (SELECT           T2.Exam_ID,           COUNT(T2.Exam_ID) AS Count_Questions      FROM           Exams T2      GROUP BY           T2.Exam_ID) T2 ON      T1.Exam_ID = T2.Exam_ID;
SELECT DISTINCT    S.Student_ID,    S.First_Name,    S.Last_Name FROM    Students S JOIN    Student_Assessments SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID JOIN    Questions_in_Exams QI ON SA.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID JOIN    Exams E ON QI.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID JOIN    Questions Q ON QI.Question_ID = Q.Question_ID ORDER BY    S.Student_ID, S.First_Name, S.Last_Name;
SELECT DISTINCT Student_Answer_ID FROM Student_Answers WHERE Student_ID IN (     SELECT Student_ID     FROM Student_Assessments     WHERE Exam_ID = (         SELECT Exam_ID         FROM Student_Assessments         WHERE Question_ID = (             SELECT Question_ID             FROM Student_Answers             WHERE Exam_ID = 1         )     ) );
SELECT      S.First_Name,      S.Last_Name,      S.Email_Adress,      S.Cell_Mobile_Phone,      S.Home_Phone FROM      Students S JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON SA.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID JOIN      Questions Q ON SA2.Question_ID = Q.Question_ID WHERE      Q.Type_of_Question_Code!= 'Database'      AND Q.Question_Text LIKE '%Exam%'  ORDER BY      Q.Question_Text,      S.First_Name,      S.Last_Name,      SA2.Date_of_Answer;
SELECT    e.Exam_Name,    e.Exam_Date,    q.Question_Text  FROM    Exams e    JOIN    (SELECT       q.Question_ID,       q.Type_of_Question_Code,       q.Question_Text     FROM       Questions     JOIN       Questions_in_Exams qi ON q.Question_ID = qi.Question_ID     WHERE       q.Type_of_Question_Code!= 'Database') q    ON e.Exam_ID = q.Exam_ID  WHERE    e.Subject_Code!= 'Database';
SELECT      e.Exam_Date,      q.Question_Text  FROM      Exams e  JOIN      (SELECT           q.Question_ID,           COUNT(*) as count       FROM           Questions_in_Exams q       GROUP BY           q.Question_ID       ORDER BY           q.Question_ID       LIMIT 1) q ON e.Exam_ID = q.Exam_ID  JOIN      Student_Answers sa ON e.Exam_ID = sa.Exam_ID AND sa.Question_ID = q.Question_ID  JOIN      Students s ON sa.Student_ID = s.Student_ID  JOIN      Valid_Answers va ON sa.Student_Answer_ID = va.Valid_Answer_ID  WHERE      va.Question_ID = 321 AND s.First_Name = 'Wilbert' AND s.Last_Name = 'Mia' AND s.Gender_MFU = 'F' ORDER BY      e.Exam_Date DESC;
SELECT    q.Type_of_Question_Code,    q.Question_Text,    a.Date_of_Answer  FROM    (SELECT     q.Type_of_Question_Code,     q.Question_Text,     COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN a.Satisfactory_YN = '1' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS Satisfactory_YN    FROM     Questions q    JOIN     Student_Answers a ON q.Question_ID = a.Question_ID) AS subquery WHERE    q.Type_of_Question_Code LIKE '%data%'    AND q.Question_Text LIKE '%data%'  ORDER BY    q.Type_of_Question_Code,    q.Question_Text;
SELECT      T1.Type_of_Question_Code,      COUNT(DISTINCT T2.Exam_ID) AS Count FROM      Questions T1 JOIN      Questions_in_Exams T2 ON T1.Type_of_Question_Code = T2.Type_of_Question_Code GROUP BY      T1.Type_of_Question_Code;
SELECT      T1.Type_of_Question_Code,      COUNT(DISTINCT T2.Question_ID) AS Count FROM      Exams E JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QI ON E.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON E.Exam_ID = SA.Exam_ID AND SA.Student_ID = E.Student_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON QI.Question_ID = SA2.Question_ID AND SA2.Student_ID = E.Student_ID JOIN      Valid_Answers VA ON QI.Question_ID = VA.Question_ID AND VA.Valid_Answer_ID = SA2.Valid_Answer_ID GROUP BY      T1.Type_of_Question_Code ORDER BY      T1.Type_of_Question_Code;
SELECT DISTINCT      S.Student_ID,      S.First_Name,      S.Last_Name,      S.Gender_MFU,      A.Student_Answer_Text FROM      Students S JOIN      Student_Answers A ON S.Student_ID = A.Student_ID WHERE      A.Comments = 'Normal';
SELECT DISTINCT      S.Student_Answer_Text FROM      Student_Answers SA JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON SA.Student_Answer_ID = SA2.Student_Answer_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA3 ON SA2.Student_ID = SA3.Student_ID JOIN      Valid_Answers VA ON SA3.Valid_Answer_ID = VA.Valid_Answer_ID WHERE      VA.Question_ID IN (         SELECT              Q.Question_ID         FROM              Questions         WHERE              Satisfactory_YN = 'Normal'     ) ORDER BY      Satisfactory_YN;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT          S1.Student_ID,          S1.Question_ID)  FROM      Student_Answers AS S1  JOIN      Student_Assessments AS S2 ON S1.Student_ID = S2.Student_ID  JOIN      Students AS S3 ON S2.Student_Answer_ID = S3.Student_ID  JOIN      Valid_Answers AS S4 ON S2.Valid_Answer_ID = S4.Valid_Answer_ID  JOIN      Questions_in_Exams AS S5 ON S2.Question_ID = S5.Question_ID  JOIN      Questions AS S6 ON S5.Exam_ID = S6.Exam_ID  WHERE      S1.Satisfactory_YN = '1'  GROUP BY      S1.Student_ID,      S1.Question_ID;
SELECT    COUNT(DISTINCT E.Exam_ID) AS Exam_ID,    COUNT(DISTINCT Q.Question_ID) AS Question_ID FROM    Exams E   JOIN Questions_in_Exams QI ON E.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID   JOIN Student_Answers SA ON E.Exam_ID = SA.Exam_ID AND SA.Student_ID = E.Student_ID   JOIN Student_Assessments S ON SA.Student_ID = S.Student_ID AND S.Valid_Answer_ID = SA.Student_Answer_ID   JOIN Valid_Answers V ON S.Valid_Answer_ID = V.Valid_Answer_ID GROUP BY    E.Exam_ID,    QI.Question_ID ORDER BY    E.Exam_ID,    QI.Question_ID DESC;
SELECT      S1.Student_ID,      S1.Student_Answer_Text,      COUNT(SA.Student_ID) AS Answer_Count FROM      Student_Answers SA JOIN      Students S1 ON SA.Student_ID = S1.Student_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON S1.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID JOIN      Exams E ON SA2.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID JOIN      Questions Q ON E.Exam_ID = Q.Exam_ID GROUP BY      S1.Student_ID, S1.Student_Answer_Text ORDER BY      Answer_Count DESC;
SELECT      q.Question_Text,      COUNT(sa.Student_Answer_ID) AS Frequency FROM      Student_Answers sa JOIN      Student_Assessments saa ON sa.Student_Answer_ID = saa.Student_Answer_ID JOIN      Questions_in_Exams qie ON saa.Exam_ID = qie.Exam_ID JOIN      Exams e ON qie.Exam_ID = e.Exam_ID JOIN      Questions q ON e.Question_ID = q.Question_ID GROUP BY      q.Question_Text ORDER BY      Frequency DESC;
SELECT    S.First_Name,    S.Last_Name  FROM    Students AS S    JOIN Student_Assessments AS SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID    JOIN Student_Answers AS SA2 ON S.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID    JOIN Questions AS Q ON SA2.Question_ID = Q.Question_ID  WHERE    Q.Type_of_Question_Code = 'Single Choice'    AND Q.Type_of_Question_Code = 'Multiple Choice'    AND Q.Type_of_Question_Code = 'Multiple Choice'    AND SA.Satisfactory_YN = '1'    AND SA2.Satisfactory_YN = '1'    AND SA2.Assessment = 'Very Good'    AND SA2.Assessment = 'Excellent'    AND SA2.Assessment = 'Very Good' ORDER BY    S.Student_ID;
SELECT      S.First_Name,      S.Date_of_Answer FROM      Student_Assessments SA2 JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON SA.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID JOIN      Students S ON SA2.Student_ID = S.Student_ID WHERE      SA2.Exam_ID IN (         SELECT              Exam_ID          FROM              Exams          GROUP BY              Exam_ID          HAVING              COUNT(*) = 1     ) ORDER BY      S.Student_ID,      S.First_Name,      SA2.Date_of_Answer;
SELECT      MAX(SA.Date_of_Answer) AS Date_of_Answer,      E.Exam_Name,      Q.Question_Text FROM      Exams E JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QI ON E.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON QI.Exam_ID = SA.Exam_ID AND QI.Question_ID = SA.Question_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA3 ON E.Exam_ID = SA3.Exam_ID AND QI.Question_ID = SA3.Question_ID GROUP BY      E.Exam_ID, QI.Exam_ID, QI.Question_ID, SA3.Exam_ID, SA3.Question_ID ORDER BY      SA3.Date_of_Answer DESC;
SELECT      S1.Student_ID,      S1.Date_of_Answer,      S1.Satisfactory_YN,      S1.Student_Answer_Text  FROM      Student_Answers S1  JOIN      Student_Assessments S2 ON S1.Student_ID = S2.Student_ID  JOIN      Questions_in_Exams Q3 ON S1.Exam_ID = Q3.Exam_ID AND S1.Question_ID = Q3.Question_ID  WHERE      S2.Comments IS NULL  ORDER BY      S1.Date_of_Answer DESC;
SELECT      S.First_Name,      S.Last_Name,      S.Gender_MFU,      S.Email_Adress,      S.Cell_Mobile_Phone,      S.Home_Phone,      COALESCE(QA.Satisfactory_YN, 'Not Satisfactory') AS Satisfactory_YN,      COALESCE(QA.Student_Answer_Text, '') AS Student_Answer_Text FROM      Students S  JOIN      Valid_Answers QA ON S.Student_ID = QA.Student_ID  JOIN      Student_Assessments SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID  JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QIE ON QA.Question_ID = QIE.Question_ID  WHERE      QIE.Exam_ID = 1  GROUP BY      S.First_Name,      S.Last_Name,      S.Gender_MFU,      S.Email_Adress,      S.Cell_Mobile_Phone,      S.Home_Phone,      QA.Satisfactory_YN,      QA.Student_Answer_Text;
SELECT      E.Exam_Name,      COUNT(DISTINCT S1.Student_ID) AS Student_Score FROM      Student_Answers S1 JOIN      Student_Assessments SA ON S1.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID JOIN      Exams E ON SA.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID JOIN      Questions Q ON E.Exam_ID = Q.Exam_ID JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QI ON Q.Question_ID = QI.Question_ID JOIN      Valid_Answers VA ON QI.Question_ID = VA.Question_ID GROUP BY      E.Exam_Name ORDER BY      Student_Score ASC LIMIT 1;
SELECT      S.First_Name, S.Last_Name  FROM      Students S  JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID  JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON S.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID  JOIN      Valid_Answers VA ON SA2.Valid_Answer_ID = VA.Valid_Answer_ID  WHERE      SA2.Satisfactory_YN = '1'      AND VA.Question_ID IN (         SELECT              Q.Question_ID          FROM              Questions_in_Exams Q          WHERE              Q.Exam_ID = 1          GROUP BY              Q.Question_ID          HAVING              COUNT(*) >= 2     ) ORDER BY      S.First_Name, S.Last_Name;
SELECT      S.First_Name  FROM      Students  WHERE      Student_Answer_ID IN (     SELECT          S1.Student_ID      FROM          Student_Assessments AS S1      JOIN          Student_Answers AS S2 ON S1.Student_ID = S2.Student_ID      JOIN          Questions_in_Exams AS Q3 ON S2.Question_ID = Q3.Question_ID      WHERE          Q3.Exam_ID IN (             SELECT                  E.Exam_ID              FROM                  Exams AS E              WHERE                  E.Subject_Code IN ('Art History', 'English')         )     GROUP BY          S1.Student_ID      HAVING          COUNT(*) >= 2     )
SELECT      V.Answering_Question.Text AS MostCommonAnswer  FROM      Valid_Answers AS V  JOIN      Questions AS Q ON V.Question_ID = Q.Question_ID  WHERE      V.Question_ID IN (         SELECT              Q1.Question_ID          FROM              Questions_in_Exams AS Q1          JOIN              Exams AS E ON Q1.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID          WHERE              E.Exam_Name = 'What is the most common valid answer text?'     )  GROUP BY      V.Question_ID  ORDER BY      COUNT(*) DESC  LIMIT 1;
SELECT    E.Exam_Name,    Q.Type_of_Question_Code,    COUNT(QA.Valid_Answer_ID) AS Valid_Answer_Count FROM    Exams E   JOIN Questions_in_Exams QI ON E.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID   JOIN Student_Answers SA ON QI.Question_ID = SA.Question_ID   JOIN Valid_Answers V ON SA.Valid_Answer_ID = V.Valid_Answer_ID GROUP BY    E.Exam_ID, QI.Question_ID ORDER BY    Valid_Answer_Count DESC;
SELECT Student_ID FROM Students WHERE Gender_MFU!= 'M' GROUP BY Student_ID ORDER BY Student_ID;
SELECT DISTINCT      S.Last_Name  FROM      Students S  JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID  WHERE      S.Gender_MFU!= 'M' AND      SA.Satisfactory_YN = 1;
SELECT      S.Gender_MFU,      COUNT(DISTINCT V.Student_ID) AS Number_of_students FROM      Students AS S JOIN      Valid_Answers AS V ON S.Student_ID = V.Student_ID JOIN      Questions_in_Exams AS Q ON V.Question_ID = Q.Question_ID JOIN      Exams AS E ON Q.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID GROUP BY      S.Gender_MFU ORDER BY      S.Gender_MFU;
SELECT      S.Gender_MFU,      COUNT(DISTINCT S.Student_ID) AS Student_Count FROM      Students S JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON S.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID JOIN      Exams E ON SA2.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QI ON SA2.Question_ID = QI.Question_ID JOIN      Valid_Answers VA ON QI.Question_ID = VA.Question_ID GROUP BY      S.Gender_MFU,      S.Student_ID ORDER BY      S.Gender_MFU,      Student_Count DESC;
SELECT      S.Last_Name  FROM      Students S  WHERE      S.Gender_MFU = 'F' OR S.Gender_MFU = 'M' ORDER BY      S.Last_Name;
SELECT    S.Last_Name  FROM    Students S  WHERE    S.Gender_MFU = 'M'    AND S.Last_Name LIKE 'F%' ORDER BY    S.Last_Name;
SELECT DISTINCT Student_Answer_ID  FROM Student_Assessments  WHERE Satisfactory_YN = '0' ORDER BY Student_Answer_ID;
SELECT DISTINCT Student_Answer_Text  FROM Student_Answers  WHERE Student_ID NOT IN (   SELECT Student_ID    FROM Student_Assessments    JOIN Student_Answers ON Student_Assments.Student_ID = Student_Answers.Student_ID   WHERE Student_Assments.Exam_ID = Exams.Exam_ID   AND Student_Assments.Question_ID IN (     SELECT Question_ID      FROM Questions_in_Exams      WHERE Exam_ID = Exams.Exam_ID   ) )
SELECT      S1.Student_ID,      S1.First_Name,      S1.Last_Name,      S1.Gender_MFU,      S1.Student_Address,      S1.Email_Adress,      S1.Cell_Mobile_Phone,      S1.Home_Phone,      S1.Satisfactory_YN,      S1.Student_Answer_Text,      S2.Comments  FROM      Student_Answers S1  INNER JOIN      Student_Assessments S2 ON S1.Student_ID = S2.Student_ID  INNER JOIN      Valid_Answers S3 ON S2.Valid_Answer_ID = S3.Valid_Answer_ID  WHERE      S1.Comments IN ('Normal', 'Absent') ORDER BY      S1.Student_ID,      S1.First_Name,      S1.Last_Name;
SELECT      S1.Student_ID,      S1.First_Name,      S1.Last_Name,      S1.Gender_MFU,      S1.Student_Address,      S1.Email_Adress,      S1.Cell_Mobile_Phone,      S1.Home_Phone FROM      Students S1 JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON S1.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID JOIN      Student_Assessments SA2 ON S1.Student_ID = SA2.Student_ID JOIN      Valid_Answers VA ON SA2.Valid_Answer_ID = VA.Valid_Answer_ID JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QIE ON SA2.Question_ID = QIE.Question_ID JOIN      Exams E ON QIE.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID WHERE      SA2.Satisfactory_YN = 'Normal'     AND QIE.Question_ID IN (321, 585) ORDER BY      S1.Student_ID;
SELECT    E.Exam_ID,    Q.Type_of_Question_Code AS Question_Type,    COUNT(DISTINCT S.Student_ID) AS Count FROM    Exams E   JOIN Questions_in_Exams QI ON E.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID   JOIN Student_Answers SA ON QI.Question_ID = SA.Question_ID   JOIN Students S ON SA.Student_ID = S.Student_ID GROUP BY    E.Exam_ID, QI.Question_ID HAVING    COUNT(DISTINCT E.Exam_ID) >= 3;
SELECT      T1.Type_of_Question_Code AS Question_Type FROM      Exams E JOIN      (SELECT Exam_ID       FROM Exams       GROUP BY Exam_ID       HAVING COUNT(*) >= 3) AS H ON E.Exam_ID = H.Exam_ID JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QI ON E.Exam_ID = QI.Exam_ID JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON QI.Question_ID = SA.Question_ID WHERE      E.Exam_ID IN (         SELECT Exam_ID          FROM Exams          GROUP BY Exam_ID          HAVING COUNT(*) >= 3     ) ORDER BY      COUNT(*) DESC;
SELECT      S.First_Name, S.Last_Name,      Q.Type_of_Question_Code, Q.Question_Text,      SA.Satisfactory_YN,      SA.Student_Answer_Text  FROM      Students S  JOIN      Student_Answers SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID  JOIN      Questions_in_Exams QI ON SA.Question_ID = QI.Question_ID  JOIN      Exams E ON QI.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID  WHERE      E.Exam_Name = 'Students'      AND QI.Type_of_Question_Code = 'Single Choice'      AND SA.Satisfactory_YN = 'Yes' ORDER BY      S.First_Name, S.Last_Name;
SELECT    S.First_Name,    S.Last_Name,    S.Gender_MFU,    S.Email_Adress,    S.Cell_Mobile_Phone,    S.Home_Phone FROM    Students S   JOIN    Student_Answers SA ON S.Student_ID = SA.Student_ID   JOIN    Valid_Answers VA ON SA.Valid_Answer_ID = VA.Valid_Answer_ID   JOIN    Questions Q ON SA.Question_ID = Q.Question_ID   JOIN    Exams E ON Q.Exam_ID = E.Exam_ID WHERE    E.Exam_Name = 'Available Information of each student'   AND E.Exam_Date = '2016-12-19'   AND Q.Question_Text = 'Available information of each student' ORDER BY    S.Student_ID;
SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Addresses AS A WHERE A.address_details LIKE '%Ofelia%' GROUP BY A.address_id ORDER BY A.address_id ASC;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT customer_id) FROM Customers;
SELECT      a.address_id,      a.address_details,      c.customer_name,      c.customer_address,      COALESCE(p.product_name, 'Unknown Product') AS product_name FROM      Addresses a  JOIN      Customers c ON a.address_id = c.address_id  JOIN      Customer_Orders co ON a.address_id = co.order_id  JOIN      Order_Items oi ON a.address_id = oi.order_id  JOIN      Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id;
SELECT    a.address_id,    a.address_details,    c.customer_name,    c.customer_address,    c.customer_phone,    c.customer_email FROM    Addresses a   INNER JOIN Customers c ON a.address_id = c.address_id   INNER JOIN Order_Items oi ON a.address_id = oi.address_id   INNER JOIN Customer_Orders co ON oi.order_id = co.order_id   INNER JOIN Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id ORDER BY    a.address_id,    c.customer_name;
SELECT      "Addresses.address_id",      "Customers.customer_id",      "Order_Items.order_id",      "Products.product_id",      "Order_Items.product_name",      "Order_Items.order_quantity" FROM      "Addresses",      "Customers",      "Order_Items",      "Products" WHERE      "Addresses.address_id" = "Customers.customer_id" AND      "Customers.customer_id" = "Order_Items.customer_id" AND      "Order_Items.product_id" = "Products.product_id";
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT customer_id)  FROM Customer_Orders;
SELECT    P.product_id,    P.product_type_code,    P.product_name FROM    Products P   JOIN    (SELECT       order_id,       product_id,       product_type_code,       product_name     FROM       Order_Items     GROUP BY       order_id,       product_id,       product_type_code,       product_name) OI   ON P.product_id = OI.product_id;
SELECT      A.address_id,      C.customer_id,      P.product_name FROM      Addresses A JOIN      Customer_Orders CO ON A.address_id = CO.customer_id JOIN      Customer_Orders CO2 ON CO.order_id = CO2.customer_id JOIN      Order_Items OI ON CO2.order_id = OI.order_id JOIN      Products P ON OI.product_id = P.product_id WHERE      CO.order_date BETWEEN '2016-05-14' AND '2016-05-14';
SELECT p.product_name FROM Products p WHERE p.product_id = (   SELECT product_id   FROM Order_Items   WHERE order_id IN (     SELECT order_id     FROM Customer_Orders     WHERE customer_id = 3   ) );
SELECT p.product_name  FROM Products p  WHERE p.product_id = (   SELECT oi.product_id    FROM Order_Items oi    JOIN Customers c ON oi.customer_id = c.customer_id    WHERE c.customer_id = 3 );
SELECT    AVG(CASE      WHEN order_id IN (SELECT order_id FROM Customer_Orders) THEN product_price      ELSE 0    END) AS min_price,   AVG(CASE      WHEN order_id IN (SELECT order_id FROM Customer_Orders) THEN product_price      ELSE 0    END) AS avg_price,   MAX(CASE      WHEN order_id IN (SELECT order_id FROM Customer_Orders) THEN product_price      ELSE 0    END) AS max_price FROM    Order_Items   JOIN Order_Items ON Order_Items.order_id = Order_Items.order_id   JOIN Customer_Orders ON Order_Items.order_id = Customer_Orders.order_id   JOIN Products ON Order_Items.product_id = Products.product_id GROUP BY    order_id;
SELECT      AVG(CASE          WHEN o.order_date BETWEEN '2016-05-14' AND '2016-05-15' THEN p.product_price          WHEN o.order_date BETWEEN '1991-02-26' AND '1991-02-27' THEN p.product_price          ELSE 0      END) AS min_price,     AVG(CASE          WHEN o.order_date BETWEEN '2000-06-26' AND '2000-06-27' THEN p.product_price          WHEN o.order_date BETWEEN '2016-05-14' AND '2016-05-15' THEN p.product_price          ELSE 0      END) AS max_price,     AVG(CASE          WHEN o.order_date BETWEEN '1991-02-26' AND '1991-02-27' THEN p.product_price          WHEN o.order_date BETWEEN '2000-06-26' AND '2000-06-27' THEN p.product_price          ELSE 0      END) AS avg_price FROM      (SELECT          o.order_id,          o.product_id,          o.product_price      FROM          Order_Items o
SELECT      AVG(CASE          WHEN p.product_type_code = 'Clothes' THEN p.product_price          ELSE 0      END) AS avg_price  FROM      (SELECT          COALESCE(p.product_price, 0) AS product_price      FROM          Products p      JOIN          Order_Items oi ON p.product_id = oi.product_id      JOIN          Customers c ON oi.customer_id = c.customer_id      JOIN          Addresses a ON c.address_id = a.address_id      WHERE          c.customer_id = 3      AND p.product_type_code = 'Clothes')      AS clothes
SELECT AVG(product_price) AS average_price FROM Products GROUP BY product_id;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT product_id FROM Products) AS num_hardware_products;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT product_id FROM Products) AS num_hardware_products FROM Customer_Orders JOIN Order_Items ON Customer_Orders.order_id = Order_Items.order_id JOIN Products ON Order_Items.product_id = Products.product_id WHERE product_type_code = 'Hardware';
SELECT    p.product_name  FROM    Products p  WHERE    p.product_price > (     SELECT AVG(pi.product_price      FROM        Order_Items oi      JOIN        Customer_Orders co        ON oi.order_id = co.order_id      JOIN        Addresses a        ON co.address_id = a.address_id      WHERE        co.order_status_code = 'Part'   )    )  ORDER BY    p.product_name DESC;
SELECT      P.product_name  FROM      Products P  WHERE      P.product_price > (         SELECT AVG(P.product_price)          FROM              Products P          WHERE              P.product_id = P.product_id     );
SELECT P.product_name  FROM Products P  WHERE P.product_type_code = 'Hardware'  AND P.product_price > (SELECT AVG(OI.product_price)                       FROM Order_Items OI                       JOIN Customer_Orders CO ON OI.order_id = CO.order_id                       JOIN Addresses A ON CO.address_id = A.address_id                       WHERE CO.order_status_code = 'Part'                       AND A.address_details LIKE '%Rueckerborough, NV%'                       OR A.address_details LIKE '%Landenshire, ND%'));
SELECT    p.product_name  FROM    Products p  WHERE    p.product_price > (     SELECT        AVG(o.product_price)      FROM        Customer_Orders o      WHERE        o.order_status_code = 'Part'   );
SELECT    p.product_name  FROM    Products p  WHERE    p.product_type_code = 'Clothes'    AND p.product_price = (SELECT MAX(p2.product_price)                          FROM Products p2                          JOIN Order_Items oi ON p2.product_id = oi.product_id                          JOIN Customer_Orders co ON oi.order_id = co.order_id)  ORDER BY    p.product_price DESC;
SELECT    c.customer_name,    c.customer_address,    AVG(p.product_price) AS average_price FROM    Customers c   JOIN Order_Items oi ON c.customer_id = oi.customer_id   JOIN Order order_i ON oi.order_id = order_i.order_id   JOIN Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY    c.customer_id, c.customer_address ORDER BY    average_price DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT      P.product_id,      P.product_name  FROM      Products P  JOIN      (SELECT           product_id,           product_type_code,           product_name       FROM           Products       WHERE           product_type_code IN ('Hardware', 'Clothes')) AS T ON P.product_id = T.product_id  WHERE      T.product_type_code = 'Hardware'  ORDER BY      P.product_price ASC;
SELECT    c.customer_id,    c.customer_name FROM    Customers c JOIN    (SELECT      customer_id,      order_id,      MIN(order_id) as min_order_id    FROM      Customer_Orders    GROUP BY      customer_id) co ON c.customer_id = co.customer_id   JOIN    (SELECT      order_id,      MIN(order_id) as min_order_id    FROM      Order_Items    GROUP BY      order_id) oi ON co.order_id = oi.order_id WHERE    co.min_order_id = oi.min_order_id;
SELECT      p.product_name,      p.product_price  FROM      Products p  WHERE      p.product_price DESC  ORDER BY      p.product_price;
SELECT    p.product_name,    SUM(o.order_quantity) AS total_quantity FROM    Products p   JOIN Order_Items oi ON p.product_id = oi.product_id   JOIN Customer_Orders co ON oi.order_id = co.order_id GROUP BY    p.product_name ORDER BY    p.product_name DESC;
SELECT    p.product_name,    p.product_price FROM    Products p WHERE    p.product_type_code = 'Hardware' ORDER BY    p.product_price ASC;
SELECT      C.customer_name,      COALESCE(O.order_date, 'N/A') AS order_date,      COALESCE(O.order_status_code, 'N/A') AS order_status_code FROM      Customers C JOIN      Customer_Orders O ON C.customer_id = O.customer_id JOIN      Order_Items OI ON O.order_id = OI.order_id JOIN      Products P ON OI.product_id = P.product_id ORDER BY      C.customer_name,      O.order_date,      O.order_status_code;
SELECT      p.product_type_code,      COUNT(DISTINCT oi.order_id) AS order_count FROM      Products p JOIN      (SELECT           customer_id,           COUNT(DISTINCT order_id) AS order_count      FROM           Order_Items      GROUP BY           customer_id) oi ON p.product_id = oi.product_id GROUP BY      p.product_type_code ORDER BY      order_count DESC;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT C.customer_id) AS total_orders,     C.customer_id,     COALESCE(O.order_id, 0) AS order_id,     COALESCE(O.order_quantity, 0) AS order_quantity,     COALESCE(P.product_id, 0) AS product_id FROM      Customer_Orders C JOIN      Order_Items O ON C.order_id = O.order_id JOIN      Products P ON O.product_id = P.product_id GROUP BY      C.customer_id, COALESCE(O.order_id, 0), COALESCE(O.order_quantity, 0), COALESCE(P.product_id, 0);
SELECT      p.product_type_code,      AVG(pi.product_price) AS avg_price FROM      Products p JOIN      Order_Items oi ON p.product_id = oi.product_id GROUP BY      p.product_type_code;

SELECT    p.product_type_code,    c.customer_name FROM    Products p   JOIN Customers c ON p.product_id = c.customer_id WHERE    p.product_type_code IN ('Hardware', 'Clothes', 'Black Jeans') GROUP BY    c.customer_name ORDER BY    c.customer_name DESC

SELECT    c.payment_method_code,    COUNT(DISTINCT COALESCE(o.order_id, 0)) AS num_customers FROM    Customers c   JOIN    Customer_Orders o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id   JOIN    Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id GROUP BY    c.payment_method_code ORDER BY    num_customers DESC;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT "Customers".customer_id) AS num_customers,     COUNT("Customers".customer_id) AS num_customers,     COUNT("Customers".customer_id) AS num_customers FROM      "Customers" AS "Customers" JOIN      "Addresses" AS "Addresses" ON "Customers".address_id = "Addresses".address_id GROUP BY      "Customers".customer_id ORDER BY      num_customers DESC;
SELECT      'Customers' AS main_table,      'payment_method_code' AS key_column,      'Customers' AS join_table,      'payment_method_code' AS join_column,      'Customers' AS group_by_column,      'Customers' AS order_by_column,      'Customers' AS filter_column,      'Customers' AS filter_group FROM      'Customers' WHERE      payment_method_code = (     SELECT          'payment_method_code' AS main_table,          'payment_method_code' AS key_column,          'Customers' AS join_table,          'payment_method_code' AS join_column,          'Customers' AS group_by_column,          'Customers' AS order_by_column,          'Customers' AS filter_column,          'Customers' AS filter_group     FROM          'Customers'     GROUP BY          'payment_method_code'     HAVING          COUNT(*) > 1     ) ORDER BY      'payment_method_code',      'Customers' ASC
SELECT      c.payment_method_code FROM      Customer_Orders co JOIN      Customer c ON co.customer_id = c.customer_id JOIN      Order_Items oi ON co.order_id = oi.order_id JOIN      Addresses a ON co.customer_address = a.address_id WHERE      co.order_status_code = 'Part';

SELECT    COALESCE(C.customer_name, 'Unknown') AS customer_name,    COALESCE(C.address_details, 'Unknown') AS customer_address,    COALESCE(C.payment_method_code, 'Unknown') AS payment_method_code FROM    Customers C   JOIN Order_Items O ON C.customer_id = O.customer_id   JOIN Addresses A ON O.address_id = A.address_id WHERE    C.customer_id = 3 ORDER BY    O.order_id DESC LIMIT 1;

SELECT      A.address_id,      A.address_details,      COALESCE(COALESCE(O.order_date, '1970-01-01'), '1970-01-01') AS order_date,      COALESCE(COALESCE(O.order_status_code, 'Unknown'), 'Unknown') AS order_status_code FROM      Addresses A JOIN      Customer_Orders O ON A.address_id = O.address_id JOIN      Order_Items OI ON O.order_id = OI.order_id JOIN      Products P ON OI.product_id = P.product_id JOIN      Customers C ON O.order_id = C.order_id
SELECT      c.customer_id,      c.customer_number,      c.customer_name,      c.customer_address,      c.customer_phone,      c.customer_email FROM      Customers c JOIN      Order_Items oi ON c.customer_id = oi.customer_id JOIN      Order order_ ON oi.order_id = order_.order_id WHERE      c.customer_id IN (1, 2, 3) ORDER BY      c.customer_id,      c.customer_number,      c.customer_address,      c.customer_phone,      c.customer_email;
SELECT      c.customer_name,      COUNT(DISTINCT COALESCE(o.order_id, 0)) AS num_orders FROM      Customers c JOIN      Customer_Orders o ON c.customer_id = o.customer_id JOIN      Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id GROUP BY      c.customer_name
SELECT      c.customer_name,      COUNT(DISTINCT cu.order_id) AS num_orders,      c.customer_address AS address FROM      Customers c JOIN      Customer_Orders cu ON c.customer_id = cu.customer_id JOIN      Order_Items oi ON cu.order_id = oi.order_id GROUP BY      c.customer_id, c.customer_address ORDER BY      c.order_id DESC;
SELECT      C.customer_id,      C.name,      C.email FROM      "Customer_Orders" C JOIN      "Order_Items" OI ON C.customer_id = OI.customer_id GROUP BY      C.customer_id, C.name, C.email ORDER BY      COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT    c.customer_id,    c.name,    c.email FROM    Customers c JOIN    (SELECT    	customer_id,    	max(order_id) as max_orders    FROM      Customer_Orders    GROUP BY       customer_id) as subquery   ON c.customer_id = subquery.max_orders JOIN    (SELECT    	customer_id,    	max(order_id) as max_orders    FROM      Customer_Orders    GROUP BY       customer_id) as subquery2   ON c.customer_id = subquery2.max_orders JOIN    (SELECT    	customer_id,    	max(order_id) as max_orders    FROM      Order_Items    GROUP BY       customer_id) as subquery3   ON c.customer_id = subquery3.max_orders JOIN    Products p   ON subquery3.product_id = p.product_id;
SELECT      COALESCE(C.customer_name, 'Unknown') AS customer_name,      COUNT(DISTINCT COALESCE(O.order_status_code, 'Unknown') AS order_status FROM      Customers C JOIN      Customer_Orders O ON C.customer_id = O.customer_id JOIN      Order_Items OI ON O.order_id = OI.order_id GROUP BY      C.customer_id, C.customer_name ORDER BY      C.customer_name,      COALESCE(O.order_status_code, 'Unknown');

SELECT    o.order_status_code,    COUNT(DISTINCT o.order_id) as order_count FROM    Customer_Orders o   JOIN Customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id   JOIN Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id   JOIN Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY    o.order_status_code ORDER BY    order_count DESC;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT customer_id) FROM Customers WHERE customer_id NOT IN (   SELECT customer_id   FROM Customer_Orders   WHERE customer_id IN (     SELECT customer_id     FROM Customer_Orders     WHERE order_id IN (       SELECT order_id       FROM Order_Items       WHERE order_id IN (         SELECT order_id         FROM Customer_Orders         WHERE customer_id = 3       )     )   ) );

SELECT DISTINCT product_name  FROM Products  WHERE product_id NOT IN (   SELECT product_id    FROM Order_Items    WHERE order_id IN (     SELECT order_id      FROM Customer_Orders      WHERE customer_id = 3   ) );
SELECT DISTINCT p.product_name FROM Products p WHERE p.product_id NOT IN (   SELECT oi.product_id   FROM Order_Items oi   JOIN Customer_Orders co ON oi.order_id = co.order_id   WHERE co.order_status_code = 'Not Ordered' );

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT o.order_id) FROM Customer_Orders o JOIN Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id JOIN Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id;

SELECT    c.customer_id,    COUNT(DISTINCT p.product_id) AS product_count FROM    Customers c   JOIN Order_Items oi ON c.customer_id = oi.customer_id   JOIN Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id GROUP BY    c.customer_id ORDER BY    c.customer_id,    COUNT(DISTINCT p.product_id) DESC;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT customer_id)  FROM Customer_Orders AS c  JOIN Addresses AS a ON c.customer_id = a.customer_id  JOIN Order_Items AS oi ON c.order_id = oi.order_id  JOIN Products AS p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id;
SELECT    COUNT(DISTINCT customer_id) AS customer_count FROM    (SELECT      customer_id,      COUNT(DISTINCT order_id) AS order_count   FROM      Customer_Orders   WHERE      order_status_code = 'Part'   GROUP BY      customer_id) AS subquery   LEFT JOIN    (SELECT      customer_id,      COUNT(DISTINCT order_id) AS order_count   FROM      Customer_Orders   WHERE      order_status_code = 'Part'   GROUP BY      customer_id) AS subquery2 ON    subquery.customer_id = subquery2.customer_id ORDER BY    customer_id,    order_id;
SELECT DISTINCT customer_id FROM Customer_Orders WHERE customer_id NOT IN (   SELECT customer_id   FROM Customer_Orders   WHERE order_id IN (     SELECT order_id     FROM Order_Items     WHERE order_id IN (       SELECT order_id       FROM Customer_Orders       WHERE customer_id = 3     )   ) );
SELECT      c.customer_id,      c.customer_name FROM      Customers c WHERE      c.customer_id NOT IN (         SELECT              o.customer_id         FROM              Customer_Orders o         JOIN              Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id     ) AND      c.customer_id NOT IN (         SELECT              ci.customer_id         FROM              Customer_Orders ci     ) ORDER BY      c.customer_id,      c.customer_id DESC;
SELECT    o.order_date,    o.order_id,    oi.product_id,    oi.product_name FROM    Orders o   JOIN Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id   JOIN Customers c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id WHERE    o.order_date > '2016-05-14' AND    oi.order_quantity > 6 AND    o.order_status_code IN ('Part', 'Part');
SELECT      COALESCE(o.order_id, c.order_id) AS order_id,     COALESCE(o.order_date, c.order_date) AS order_date FROM      (SELECT          COALESCE(o.order_id, c.order_id) AS order_id,         COALESCE(o.order_date, c.order_date) AS order_date     FROM          Customer_Orders o     JOIN          Customer_Orders c ON o.customer_id = c.customer_id     JOIN          Order_Items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id     JOIN          Products p ON oi.product_id = p.product_id     WHERE          o.order_quantity > 6 OR          o.order_date > '2016-05-14 00:00:00'     GROUP BY          o.order_id, o.order_date     ORDER BY          order_date DESC ) AS subquery
SELECT      COUNT(*) AS "Number of Buildings" FROM      building WHERE      Region_ID = 1;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT b.Building_ID) AS Count FROM      building b WHERE      b.Completed_Year = 1983
SELECT      b.Name,      COUNT(DISTINCT b.Number_of_Stories) AS Number_of_Stories FROM      building b JOIN      region r ON b.Region_ID = r.Region_ID GROUP BY      b.Name ORDER BY      Number_of_Stories ASC;
SELECT      b.Name,      COUNT(b."Number_of_Stories") AS num_stories FROM      building b JOIN      region r ON b."Region_ID" = r."Region_ID" GROUP BY      b.Name ORDER BY      num_stories ASC;
SELECT      region.Name,      region.Capital,      region.Area,      region.Population FROM      region ORDER BY      region.Area DESC;
SELECT      b.Name,      b.Address  FROM      building b  WHERE      b.Completed_Year = ( SELECT MAX(CASE WHEN Completed_Year = b.Completed_Year THEN Completed_Year ELSE 0 END)                          FROM building b                          JOIN region r ON b.Region_ID = r.Region_ID                         GROUP BY b.Region_ID                          ORDER BY b.Completed_Year DESC                         LIMIT 1 );

SELECT      C.name,      V.Type_of_powertrain,      SUM(V.Annual_fuel_cost) AS Annual_fuel_cost FROM      Customers C JOIN      Renting_history RH ON C.id = RH.customer_id JOIN      Vehicles V ON RH.vehicles_id = V.id WHERE      RH.vehicles_id IN (SELECT vehicles_id FROM Renting_history WHERE year = 2013 OR year = 2014) GROUP BY      C.name,      V.Type_of_powertrain ORDER BY      Annual_fuel_cost DESC;
SELECT      C.name,      V.Type_of_powertrain,      SUM(V.Cost_per_25_miles) AS Total_fuel_cost FROM      Customers C  JOIN      Renting_history RH ON C.id = RH.customer_id  JOIN      Vehicles V ON RH.vehicles_id = V.id WHERE      RH.year BETWEEN 2013 AND 2014 GROUP BY      C.name,      V.Type_of_powertrain ORDER BY      Total_fuel_cost DESC;
SELECT      "Vehicles".Type_of_powertrain,      "Vehicles".Combined_fuel_economy_rate,      "Vehicles".City_fuel_economy_rate,      "Vehicles".Highway_fuel_economy_rate FROM      "Vehicles" WHERE      "Vehicles".Type_of_powertrain = 'Electric'     AND "Vehicles".Combined_fuel_economy_rate > 0     AND "Vehicles".City_fuel_economy_rate > 0     AND "Vehicles".Highway_fuel_economy_rate > 0     AND "Vehicles".Cost_per_25_miles > 0     AND "Vehicles".Annual_fuel_cost > 0 ORDER BY      "Vehicles".Combined_fuel_economy_rate DESC;
SELECT      "Vehicles".id,      "Vehicles".name,      "Vehicles".Type_of_powertrain,      "Vehicles".Combined_fuel_economy_rate,      "Vehicles".City_fuel_economy_rate,      "Vehicles".Highway_fuel_economy_rate,      "Vehicles".Cost_per_25_miles,      "Vehicles".Annual_fuel_cost FROM      "Vehicles" WHERE      "Vehicles".Type_of_powertrain IN (SELECT "Type_of_powertrain" FROM "Discount") AND      "Vehicles".Combined_fuel_economy_rate > 0 AND      "Vehicles".City_fuel_economy_rate > 0 AND      "Vehicles".Highway_fuel_economy_rate > 0 AND      "Vehicles".Cost_per_25_miles > 0 AND      "Vehicles".Annual_fuel_cost > 0 ORDER BY      "Vehicles".Combined_fuel_economy_rate DESC;
SELECT    V.name AS "Type_of_powertrain",    COUNT(*) AS "Number_of_vehicles" FROM    Vehicles V   JOIN Renting_history RH ON V.id = RH.vehicles_id   JOIN Discount D ON RH.discount_id = D.id   JOIN Customers C ON RH.customer_id = C.id GROUP BY    V.id, V.name, C.id, D.id ORDER BY    V.id, C.id, D.id;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT Customers.name) AS customers_count,     COUNT(DISTINCT Discount.name) AS discount_count,     COUNT(DISTINCT Vehicles.name) AS vehicles_count FROM      Customers JOIN      Renting_history ON Customers.id = Renting_history.customer_id JOIN      Discount ON Renting_history.discount_id = Discount.id JOIN      Vehicles ON Renting_history.vehicles_id = Vehicles.id GROUP BY      Customers.name, Discount.name, Vehicles.name;
SELECT      "Vehicles".Type_of_powertrain FROM "Vehicles" GROUP BY "Vehicles".Type_of_powertrain ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT    COUNT(DISTINCT C.name) AS most_common_powertrain  FROM    Customers C    JOIN Renting_history RH ON C.id = RH.customer_id    JOIN Discount D ON RH.discount_id = D.id    JOIN Vehicles V ON RH.vehicles_id = V.id  WHERE    V.Type_of_powertrain = 'Electric'  GROUP BY    C.name, D.name, V.name  ORDER BY    COUNT(*) DESC;

SELECT      V.name AS Vehicle_name,      D.name AS Discount_name,      C.name AS Customer_name,      AVG(V.annual_fuel_cost) AS Average_fuel_cost FROM      Vehicles V JOIN      Renting_history R ON V.id = R.vehicles_id JOIN      Discount D ON R.discount_id = D.id JOIN      Customers C ON R.customer_id = C.id GROUP BY      V.name, D.name, C.name ORDER BY      Average_fuel_cost DESC;
SELECT      R.name AS customer_name,      V.name AS vehicle_name,      AVG(V.Cost_per_25_miles) AS average_fuel_cost FROM      Renting_history R JOIN      Vehicles V ON R.vehicles_id = V.id GROUP BY      R.name, V.name ORDER BY      R.name, V.name;
SELECT      name,      AVG(membership_credit) AS average_membership_credit FROM      Customers GROUP BY      name ORDER BY      average_membership_credit DESC;
SELECT      C.id,      C.name,      C.age,      C.membership_credit FROM      Customers C JOIN      Renting_history R1 ON C.id = R1.customer_id JOIN      Discount D1 ON R1.discount_id = D1.id JOIN      Vehicles V1 ON R1.vehicles_id = V1.id WHERE      D1.name = 'no discount'     AND D1.id IN (         SELECT              D.id         FROM              Discount D         JOIN              Vehicles V ON D.id = V.id         WHERE              V.name = '20% off'     )     AND D1.id IN (         SELECT              D.id         FROM              Discount D         JOIN              Vehicles V ON D.id = V.id         WHERE              V.name = '40'     )
SELECT C.name, C.age FROM Customers C WHERE C.membership_credit = (SELECT MAX(D.membership_credit) FROM Discount D);
SELECT      C.name,      C.age,      COUNT(DISTINCT RH.vehicles_id) as membership_credit FROM      Customers C JOIN      Renting_history RH ON C.id = RH.customer_id JOIN      Vehicles V ON RH.vehicles_id = V.id GROUP BY      C.id, C.age ORDER BY      C.age;
SELECT AVG(Customers.age) AS average_age FROM Customers WHERE Customers.membership_credit > (SELECT AVG(membership_credit) FROM Customers);

SELECT      Customers.name,      Discount.name,      COUNT(*) AS total_discounts FROM      Customers JOIN      Renting_history ON Customers.id = Renting_history.customer_id JOIN      Discount ON Renting_history.discount_id = Discount.id GROUP BY      Customers.name,      Discount.name
SELECT      c.name,      d.name AS discount_name,      COUNT(*) AS total_discounts FROM      Customers c JOIN      Renting_history rh ON c.id = rh.customer_id JOIN      Discounts d ON rh.discount_id = d.id GROUP BY      c.id, d.id ORDER BY      rh.total_hours DESC WHERE      d.id = 2;

SELECT      c.name,      SUM(rh.total_hours) AS total_hours FROM      Customers c JOIN      Renting_history rh ON c.id = rh.customer_id JOIN      Vehicles v ON rh.vehicles_id = v.id JOIN      Discount d ON v.id = d.id GROUP BY      c.name ORDER BY      total_hours DESC;
SELECT C.name FROM Customers C WHERE C.membership_credit = 0 LEFT JOIN Vehicles V ON C.id = V.id WHERE V.id IS NULL;
SELECT      V.name FROM      Vehicles WHERE      V.id NOT IN (         SELECT              RH.vehicles_id         FROM              Renting_history RH         JOIN              Customers C ON RH.customer_id = C.id         WHERE              C.membership_credit = 0     )
SELECT      c.name  FROM      Customers c WHERE      c.id IN (     SELECT          customer_id      FROM          Renting_history rh      GROUP BY          customer_id      HAVING          COUNT(DISTINCT rh.id) >= 2     ) ORDER BY      c.name DESC;
SELECT      c.name,      COUNT(*) AS total_hours FROM      Customers c JOIN      Renting_history rh ON c.id = rh.customer_id JOIN      Vehicles v ON rh.vehicles_id = v.id JOIN      Discount d ON rh.discount_id = d.id GROUP BY      c.name HAVING      COUNT(*) >= 2;
SELECT      c.name,      v."Model_year" FROM      Renting_history rh,      Customers c,      Vehicles v WHERE      rh.customer_id = c.id AND      rh.discount_id = c.id AND      rh.vehicles_id = v.id AND      rh.total_hours > 0 ORDER BY      v."Model_year";
SELECT      c.name,      v.name AS vehicle_name FROM      Customers c JOIN      (         SELECT customer_id, COUNT(*) as total_hours          FROM Renting_history          GROUP BY customer_id      ) rh ON c.id = rh.customer_id JOIN      Vehicles v ON rh.total_hours > 1000 ORDER BY      COUNT(*) DESC;
SELECT      R.name AS vehicle_name,      COUNT(DISTINCT R.total_hours) AS total_hours FROM      Renting_history RH JOIN      Vehicles V ON RH.vehicles_id = V.id GROUP BY      R.name ORDER BY      total_hours DESC;
SELECT      V.name AS vehicle_name,      COUNT(*) AS total_hours FROM      Renting_history R JOIN      Vehicles V ON R.vehicles_id = V.id GROUP BY      V.name ORDER BY      total_hours DESC;

SELECT      s.Neighborhood  FROM      store s  JOIN      stock st ON s.Store_ID = st.Store_ID  WHERE      st.Headphone_ID IN (1, 2, 3)  AND      s.Store_ID = (         SELECT              Store_ID          FROM              stock          WHERE              Headphone_ID = 1          OR          Headphone_ID = 2          OR          Headphone_ID = 3     );
SELECT      s.Name AS Store_Name,      COUNT(*) AS Headphones FROM      stock s LEFT JOIN      headphone h ON s.Headphone_ID = h.Headphone_ID GROUP BY      s.Name ORDER BY      Headphones DESC LIMIT 1;
SELECT    s.Name,    SUM(s.Quantity) AS Total_Quantity FROM    stock s   JOIN    headphone h ON s.Headphone_ID = h.Headphone_ID GROUP BY    s.Name ORDER BY    Total_Quantity DESC;
SELECT s.Name FROM store s WHERE s.Store_ID NOT IN (   SELECT t.Headphone_ID   FROM stock s1   JOIN headphone h ON s1.Headphone_ID = h.Headphone_ID );
SELECT s.Name  FROM store s  WHERE NOT EXISTS (     SELECT 1      FROM stock      WHERE s.Headphone_ID = stock.Headphone_ID )
SELECT h.Model  FROM headphone h  WHERE h.Headphone_ID NOT IN (   SELECT s.Headphone_ID    FROM stock s  );
SELECT      h.Headphone_ID,      h.Model,      h.Class,      s.Name FROM      headphone h WHERE      NOT EXISTS (         SELECT 1          FROM              stock s          WHERE              s.Headphone_ID = h.Headphone_ID     )
SELECT    h.Model,    s.Name AS Store_Name,    MAX(s.Quantity) AS Max_Quantity FROM    headphone h   JOIN stock s ON h.Headphone_ID = s.Headphone_ID GROUP BY    h.Model, s.Name ORDER BY    h.Model, s.Name, MAX(s.Quantity) DESC;
SELECT      H.Headphone_ID,      H.Model  FROM      headphone H  JOIN      stock S ON H.Headphone_ID = S.Headphone_ID  WHERE      S.Quantity > 0  ORDER BY      S.Quantity DESC;
SELECT COUNT(*)  FROM headphone  WHERE Headphone_ID IN (     SELECT Headphone_ID      FROM stock      WHERE Headphone_ID = Headphone_ID );
SELECT      SUM(CASE WHEN Headphone_ID IN (SELECT Headphone_ID FROM stock WHERE Store_ID = 1) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Total_Headphones FROM headphone WHERE Headphone_Driver-matched_dB > 0 GROUP BY Headphone_Model, Headphone_Class ORDER BY Headphone_Price DESC;
SELECT s.Neighborhood  FROM      stock s  WHERE      s.Headphone_ID NOT IN (SELECT Headphone_ID FROM headphone);
SELECT      s.Neighborhood,      COUNT(*) AS Count FROM      stock s WHERE      s.Headphone_ID NOT IN (         SELECT Headphone_ID          FROM headphone          WHERE Model = 'iGrado'     ) GROUP BY      s.Neighborhood ORDER BY      Count DESC;

SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT paper_id)  FROM      Paper  WHERE      paper_id IN (SELECT paper_id FROM Citation)      AND paper_id IN (SELECT paper_id FROM Affiliation)      AND paper_id IN (SELECT paper_id FROM Author_list)      AND paper_id IN (SELECT paper_id FROM Paper)      AND cited_paper_id IN (SELECT cited_paper_id FROM Citation)  ORDER BY      paper_id,      affiliation_id,      author_id,      title,      year;
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT p.paper_id) FROM      Citation JOIN      Citation_list ON Citation.cited_paper_id = Citation_list.paper_id JOIN      Author ON Citation_list.author_id = Author.author_id JOIN      Paper ON Citation.paper_id = Paper.paper_id JOIN      Affiliation ON Citation.affiliation_id = Affiliation.affiliation_id;

SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT p.year) AS year_count FROM      Paper p JOIN      Citation c ON p.paper_id = c.paper_id JOIN      Author_list al ON p.paper_id = al.paper_id JOIN      Affiliation a ON al.affiliation_id = a.affiliation_id WHERE      c.cited_paper_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY      p.year;

SELECT      P.property_name,      P.property_description,      P.garage_yn,      P.parking_lots,      P.price_min,      P.price_max,      P.other_property_details FROM      Properties P JOIN      Ref_Room_Types RT ON P.room_type_code = RT.room_type_code JOIN      Ref_Property_Types RP ON P.property_type_code = RP.property_type_code JOIN      Ref_Age_Categories AC ON P.property_type_code = AC.property_type_code JOIN      Ref_User_Categories UC ON P.property_type_code = UC.property_type_code JOIN      Rooms R ON P.property_id = R.property_id JOIN      User_Property_History UPH ON P.property_id = UPH.property_id JOIN      User_Searches US ON P.property_id = US.property_id WHERE      P.property_type_code = 'Over 60' ORDER BY      P.property_id;
SELECT      pf.property_feature_description  FROM      Ref_Property_Types rt  INNER JOIN      Ref_Age_Categories rc  ON      rt.property_type_code = rc.age_category_code  INNER JOIN      Rooms r  ON      rc.property_id = r.property_id  INNER JOIN      Property_Photos p  ON      r.room_number = p.room_number  INNER JOIN      Property_Features pf  ON      p.property_id = pf.property_id  WHERE      r.room_type_code = 'Over 60'  ORDER BY      pf.feature_value DESC;
SELECT      P.property_name,      COUNT(PF.property_id) AS room_count FROM      Properties P JOIN      Properties_Photos PH ON P.property_id = PH.property_id JOIN      Rooms R ON PH.property_id = R.property_id GROUP BY      P.property_name ORDER BY      room_count DESC;

SELECT    "Boxes".Code,    "Boxes".Contents,    "Boxes".Value,    "Warehouses".Code,    "Warehouses".Location FROM    "Boxes"    JOIN "Warehouses" ON "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT    "Warehouses." "Code",    COUNT(*) AS "Total Boxes",    "Warehouses." "Location"  FROM    "Warehouses"  GROUP BY    "Warehouses." "Code"  ORDER BY    "Warehouses." "Code";
SELECT      B.Code,      AVG(B.Value) AS Average,      MAX(B.Value) AS MaxValue,      MAX(CASE WHEN B.Warehouse = 1 THEN B.Value END) AS MaxWarehouse,      MAX(CASE WHEN B.Warehouse = 2 THEN B.Value END) AS MaxWarehouse2 FROM      Boxes B GROUP BY      B.Code;
SELECT      W.Code,      AVG(B.Value) AS Average,      MAX(B.Value) AS Max,      COUNT(B.Code) AS Total FROM      Boxes B JOIN      Warehouses W ON B.Warehouse = W.Code GROUP BY      W.Code;
SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN "Boxes".Contents > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS TotalBoxes FROM "Boxes" AS "B"   INNER JOIN "Warehouses" AS "W" ON "B".Warehouse = "W".Code;
SELECT      SUM(CASE WHEN "Boxes".Value > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Total FROM      "Boxes"      JOIN "Warehouses" ON "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT      W.Location AS WarehouseLocation,      MAX(B.Value) AS MaxValue FROM      Boxes B JOIN      Warehouses W ON B.Warehouse = W.Code;
SELECT B.Code, B.Value, W.Location FROM Boxes B JOIN Warehouses W ON B.Warehouse = W.Code ORDER BY B.Value DESC;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      COUNT(*) as "Boxes"  FROM      "Boxes"  INNER JOIN      "Warehouses"  ON      "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      "Boxes".Contents,      "Boxes".Value,      "Warehouses".Code,      "Warehouses".Location FROM      "Boxes"      JOIN "Warehouses" ON "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT DISTINCT COUNT(DISTINCT "Boxes".Code) FROM Boxes;
SELECT DISTINCT Warehouse FROM Boxes WHERE Contents = 'Rocks';
SELECT    B.Code,    W.Location  FROM    Boxes B    JOIN Warehouses W ON B.Warehouse = W.Code;
SELECT      Boxes.Code,      Boxes.Contents,      Warehouses.Location AS WarehouseLocation FROM      Boxes JOIN      Warehouses ON Boxes.Warehouse = Warehouses.Code;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code FROM      "Boxes" INNER JOIN      "Warehouses"  ON      "Boxes".Code = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      "Boxes".Contents,      "Boxes".Value,      "Warehouses".Code,      "Warehouses".Location FROM      "Boxes"  JOIN      "Warehouses"  ON      "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT      Warehouse,      COUNT(*) AS BoxCount FROM      Boxes GROUP BY      Warehouse ORDER BY      BoxCount DESC;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      "Warehouses".Code AS Warehouse FROM      "Boxes" AS "Boxes" JOIN      "Warehouses" AS "Warehouses" ON      "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      "Boxes".Contents,      "Boxes".Value,      "Warehouses".Code,      "Warehouses".Location FROM      "Boxes"  JOIN      "Warehouses"  ON      "Boxes".Code = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT      'Boxes' AS MainTable,      'Boxes' AS KeyColumns,      'Warehouses' AS JoinTable,      'Warehouses' AS JoinCondition,      'Boxes' AS Filtering,      'Boxes' AS Grouping,      'Boxes' AS Ordering FROM      Boxes AS MainTable JOIN      Warehouses AS JoinTable ON MainTable.Warehouse = JoinTable.Code;
SELECT      Boxes.Code,      Boxes.Contents,      Warehouses.Code AS WarehouseCode FROM      Boxes WHERE      Boxes.Value > 250.0 UNION ALL SELECT      Warehouses.Code,      Warehouses.Contents,      Warehouses.Code AS WarehouseCode FROM      Warehouses WHERE      Warehouses.Code NOT IN (SELECT Boxes.Code FROM Boxes);
SELECT      "Boxes".Code FROM      "Boxes" AS "Boxes" WHERE      "Boxes".Value > "Warehouses".Capacity;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      "Boxes".Contents,      "Boxes".Value,      "Warehouses".Code,      "Warehouses".Location FROM      "Boxes"  WHERE      "Warehouses".Code NOT IN (     SELECT          "Boxes".Code      FROM          "Boxes"      WHERE          "Warehouses".Code = "Chicago"     ) ORDER BY      "Boxes".Value DESC;
SELECT      "Boxes".Code,      "Boxes".Contents,      "Boxes".Value,      "Warehouses".Code,      "Warehouses".Location FROM      "Boxes"      JOIN "Warehouses" ON "Boxes".Warehouse = "Warehouses".Code;
SELECT    u.University_Name,    u.City,    u.State  FROM    university u    JOIN major_ranking mr ON u.University_ID = mr.University_ID    JOIN overall_ranking or ON mr.Rank = or.University_ID  ORDER BY    u.University_Name,    mr.Rank;
SELECT      u.University_Name,      m.Major_Name,      u.State FROM      major m JOIN      university u ON m.Major_ID = u.University_ID JOIN      overall_ranking o ON m.Major_ID = o.University_ID WHERE      u.University_Name IN (         SELECT              university_name          FROM              university          GROUP BY              university_name          ORDER BY              reputation_point DESC     ) ORDER BY      u.State,      u.University_Name;
SELECT COUNT(*)  FROM major_ranking AS mr  JOIN university AS u ON mr.University_ID = u.University_ID  WHERE u.University_Name IN ('Illinois', 'Ohio');
SELECT COUNT(*)  FROM major  JOIN major_ranking ON major.Major_ID = major_ranking.Major_ID  JOIN overall_ranking ON major_ranking.University_ID = overall_ranking.University_ID  WHERE major.Major_Name IN ('Illinois', 'Ohio');
SELECT      major.Major_Name,      COUNT(*) AS enrollment,      AVG(major_ranking.Reputation_point) AS average_reputation,      MIN(major_ranking.Reputation_point) AS min_reputation,      MAX(major_ranking.Reputation_point) AS max_reputation FROM major JOIN major_ranking ON major.Major_ID = major_ranking.Major_ID JOIN overall_ranking ON major_ranking.University_ID = overall_ranking.University_ID GROUP BY major.Major_Name;
SELECT      major.Major_Name,      AVG(major_ranking.Reputation_point) AS Average_Reputation,      MIN(major_ranking.Reputation_point) AS Min_Reputation,      MAX(major_ranking.Reputation_point) AS Max_Reputation FROM      major  JOIN      major_ranking ON major.Major_ID = major_ranking.Major_ID GROUP BY      major.Major_Name ORDER BY      Average_Reputation DESC;
SELECT      u.University_Name FROM      university u WHERE      u.Enrollment > (SELECT AVG(Enrollment) FROM major_ranking) ORDER BY      u.University_Name DESC GROUP BY      u.University_Name;

SELECT      P.Name,      H.`EmployeeID`,      H.`PlanetID`,      P.`PackageNumber`,      P.`Weight`,      C.AccountNumber FROM      Has_Clearance H  INNER JOIN      Package P ON H.`PlanetID` = P.PlanetID  INNER JOIN      Client C ON H.`EmployeeID` = C.AccountNumber WHERE      P.`Weight` IS NOT NULL AND C.AccountNumber IS NOT NULL ORDER BY      P.`Weight`,      C.AccountNumber;
SELECT    C.Name AS ClientName,    E.Name AS EmployeeName,    H.Has_clearance.Level AS ClearanceLevel,    P.PlanetID AS PlanetID FROM    Client C   JOIN Employee E ON C.AccountNumber = E.EmployeeID   JOIN Has_Clearance H ON E.EmployeeID = H.Employee   JOIN Package P ON H.Planet = P.PlanetID WHERE    C.AccountNumber = (     SELECT        MAX(C.AccountNumber)     FROM        Client C     GROUP BY        C.AccountNumber   ) ORDER BY    H.Has_clearance.Level DESC;
SELECT SUM(P.Weight) AS TotalWeight FROM Package AS P JOIN Has_Clearance AS H ON P.Sender = H.Employee AND P.Recipient = H.Planet JOIN Client AS C ON H.Recipient = C.AccountNumber WHERE C.AccountNumber = 1 AND H.Level = 2;
SELECT SUM(T1.`Weight` AS TotalWeight) FROM Package AS T1 INNER JOIN Has_Clearance AS T2 ON T1.Sender = T2.Employee AND T1.`PackageNumber` = T2.`PackageNumber` WHERE T2.`Planet` IS NOT NULL;
SELECT    "Client".Name,    "Employee".Name,    "Has_Clearance".Employee,    "Has_Clearance".Planet,    "Has_Clearance".Level,    "Package".PackageNumber,    "Package".Contents,    "Package".Weight,    "Planet".Name,    "Planet".Coordinates FROM    "Client"    JOIN "Has_Clearance" ON "Client".AccountNumber = "Has_Clearance".Employee    JOIN "Employee" ON "Has_Clearance".Employee = "Employee".EmployeeID    JOIN "Package" ON "Has_Clearance".Shipment = "Package".Shipment    JOIN "Planet" ON "Has_Clearance".Planet = "Planet".PlanetID;
SELECT    e.Name AS Amy_Wong FROM    Employee e   JOIN Has_Clearance hc ON e.EmployeeID = hc.Employee   JOIN Package p ON hc.Employee = p.Sender AND p.Recipient = e.EmployeeID   JOIN Planet p ON p.PlanetID = p.PlanetID WHERE    e.Name = 'Amy Wong'

SELECT e.Name, e.Position, e.Salary FROM Employee e JOIN Has_Clearance hc ON e.EmployeeID = hc.Employee WHERE e.Name = 'Turanga Leela';
SELECT AVG(Salary) FROM Employee;
SELECT AVG(Salary)  FROM Employee  WHERE EmployeeID IN (   SELECT EmployeeID    FROM Has_Clearance    JOIN Package ON Has_Clearance.Employee = Employee    JOIN Client ON Has_Clearance.Client = Client    WHERE Client.AccountNumber = 1 ) GROUP BY PackageNumber  ORDER BY PackageNumber;
SELECT    e.Name,    p.Name AS PlanetName,    COUNT(DISTINCT p.PlanetID) AS PlanetCount FROM    Employee e    JOIN Package p ON e.EmployeeID = p.Sender GROUP BY    e.Name,    p.Name ORDER BY    e.Name,    p.Name;
SELECT    "Client".Name AS ClearanceLevel,    "Has_Clearance".Level AS ClearanceLevel FROM    "Client" AS "Client"   INNER JOIN    "Has_Clearance" AS "Has_Clearance" ON "Client".AccountNumber = "Has_Clearance".Employee   INNER JOIN    "Planet" AS "Planet" ON "Has_Clearance".Planet = "Planet".PlanetID   INNER JOIN    "Package" AS "Package" ON "Has_Clearance".Sender = "Package".Sender   INNER JOIN    "Shipment" AS "Shipment" ON "Package".Shipment = "Shipment".ShipmentID   INNER JOIN    "Has_Clearance".Recipient ON "Shipment".Recipient = "Has_Clearance".Employee ORDER BY    "Client".Name, "Has_Clearance".Level;
SELECT      P.Name AS PlanetName,      COUNT(P.PlanetID) AS TotalWeight FROM      Planet P JOIN      Has_Clearance HC ON P.PlanetID = HC.PlanetID JOIN      Package P ON HC.PackageNumber = P.PackageNumber GROUP BY      P.PlanetID ORDER BY      P.PlanetID DESC;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT Package.PackageNumber)  FROM Has_Clearance  JOIN Employee  ON Has_Clearance.Employee = Employee.EmployeeID  JOIN Package  ON Has_Clearance.PlanetID = Package.PlanetID;
SELECT    P.PlanetID,    P.Name,    COUNT(P.PlanetID) AS PackageNumberCount FROM    Package P   JOIN Planet P ON P.PlanetID = P.PlanetID GROUP BY    P.PlanetID,    P.Name ORDER BY    P.PlanetID;
SELECT    e.Name,    p.Name AS PlanetName,    COUNT(DISTINCT p.PlanetID) AS PlanetCount FROM    Employee e    JOIN Has_Clearance hc ON e.EmployeeID = hc.Employee    JOIN Package p ON hc.PlanetID = p.PlanetID    JOIN Shipment s ON p.ShipmentID = s.Shipment    JOIN Planet p ON s.PlanetID = p.PlanetID  WHERE    e.EmployeeID = 1  GROUP BY    e.Name, p.Name, s.ShipmentID, p.PlanetID;
SELECT    p.PlanetID,   p.Name,   COUNT(DISTINCT h.ClearanceID) AS ClearanceCount FROM    Planet p   JOIN Has_Clearance hc ON p.PlanetID = hc.Planet   JOIN Employee e ON hc.Employee = e.EmployeeID   JOIN Package pkg ON hc.Clearance = pkg.Sender AND pkg.Recipient = p.AccountNumber GROUP BY    p.PlanetID, p.Name ORDER BY    p.PlanetID;
SELECT      H.`EmployeeID`,      P.`PlanetID`,      H.`Employee` AS `Employee Name',      H.`Planet` AS `Planet Name',      H.`Level` AS `Level',      P.`PackageNumber`,      P.`Contents`,      P.`Weight`,      H.`Sender` AS `Sender Name',      H.`Recipient` AS `Recipient Name' FROM      Has_Clearance H  JOIN      Employee E ON H.`Employee` = E.`EmployeeID'  JOIN      Package P ON H.`Package` = P.`PackageNumber' JOIN      Planet P ON P.`PlanetID` = P.`PlanetID' JOIN      Shipment S ON H.`Sender` = S.`SenderID' WHERE      H.`Sender` = C.`AccountNumber'
SELECT      COUNT(DISTINCT p.PlanetID) AS Count_Sent_Packages,     COUNT(DISTINCT p.PlanetID) AS Count_Received_Packages FROM      Has_Clearance hc JOIN      Employee e ON hc.Employee = e.EmployeeID JOIN      Client c ON hc.Sender = c.AccountNumber JOIN      Package p ON hc.Package = p.PackageNumber JOIN      Shipment s ON p.Sender = s.Shipment JOIN      Planet p ON s.PlanetID = p.PlanetID WHERE      c.AccountNumber = 2 AND e.Name = 'Ogden Wernstrom' GROUP BY      p.PlanetID;
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.PackageNumber)  FROM Has_Clearance AS T1  JOIN Package AS T2 ON T1.Sender = T2.Sender  JOIN Client AS T3 ON T1.Employee = T3.AccountNumber  WHERE T1.Level = 3 AND T3.AccountNumber = 2;
SELECT    P.Name AS PlanetName,    P.Name AS PlanetName,    SUM(P.Weight) AS TotalWeight FROM    Planet P    JOIN Has_Clearance HC ON P.PlanetID = HC.PlanetID    JOIN Package P2 ON HC.EmployeeID = P2.EmployeeID    JOIN Shipment S ON P2.ShipmentID = S.ShipmentID    JOIN Client C ON S.Recipient = C.AccountNumber  GROUP BY    P.Name,    P.Name,    S.ShipmentID,    P2.PackageNumber ORDER BY    TotalWeight DESC;
SELECT      P.Name AS PackageContents,      P.Weight,      H.Level  FROM      (SELECT          P.PackageNumber,          P.Weight,          P.Sender,          P.Recipient,          H.ClearanceLevel      FROM          Package P      JOIN          Has_Clearance H      ON          P.Sender = H.Employee      JOIN          Client C      ON          P.Recipient = C.AccountNumber) AS PackageContents  WHERE      C.AccountNumber = 1;
SELECT    p.PackageNumber,    p.Weight  FROM    Package    JOIN Planet p ON p.PlanetID = p.PlanetID    JOIN Has_Clearance hc ON p.PlanetID = hc.Planet    JOIN Client c ON hc.Recipient = c.AccountNumber  WHERE    c.Name = 'John'  ORDER BY    p.Weight DESC;
SELECT    p.PackageNumber,    p.Weight FROM    Package p WHERE    p.Sender = (SELECT EmployeeID FROM Employee WHERE Name = 'John') OR p.Sender = (SELECT EmployeeID FROM Employee WHERE Name = 'John');

SELECT      P.PackageNumber,      P.Weight  FROM      Package  WHERE      PackageNumber = (         SELECT              MIN(PackageNumber)          FROM              Package          GROUP BY              PackageNumber)  ORDER BY      PackageNumber ASC;

SELECT      C.Name,      COUNT(P.PackageNumber) AS NumberOfPackages FROM      Client C JOIN      Package P ON C.AccountNumber = P.Sender GROUP BY      C.Name ORDER BY      NumberOfPackages DESC;

SELECT    p.Name AS PlanetName,    AVG(e.Salary) AS avg_salary,    h.ClearanceID,    s.ShipmentID,    p.PlanetID FROM    Planet p    JOIN    (SELECT       PlanetID,       AVG(Salary) AS avg_salary     FROM       Employee     GROUP BY       PlanetID     HAVING       Salary > 5000) AS subquery    ON p.PlanetID = subquery.PlanetID    JOIN    Has_Clearance h    ON h.Employee = p.Employee    JOIN    Shipment s    ON s.PlanetID = h.PlanetID    JOIN    Client c    ON s.Recipient = c.AccountNumber GROUP BY    p.PlanetID,    p.Name,    h.ClearanceID,    s.ShipmentID,    p.PlanetID ORDER BY    p.PlanetID;

SELECT    a.author_name,    d.document_name,    d.document_description,    p.process_name,    p.process_description,    d.document_id,    d.document_status_code FROM    Authors a    INNER JOIN Documents d ON a.author_name = d.author_name   INNER JOIN Documents_Processes dp ON d.document_id = dp.document_id   INNER JOIN Process_Outcomes po ON dp.process_id = po.process_id   INNER JOIN Process_Status p ON po.process_code = p.process_code   INNER JOIN Staff_in_Processes si ON d.document_id = si.document_id AND si.process_id = p.process_id   INNER JOIN Staff s ON si.staff_id = s.staff_id WHERE    p.process_status_code = 'ct';
